def SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.EF_lit_chi : {N : ℕ} →
  [NeZero N] → (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) → χ.IsPrimitive → χ ≠ 1 → Prop :=
fun {N} [NeZero N] χ hχ h1 =>
  ∀ (k : ℝ → ℂ),
    ContDiff ℝ 2 k →
      HasCompactSupport k →
        (Summable fun ρ =>
            ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).mult ↑ρ) * Zeta23.paperFT k (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ)) ∧
          ∑' (ρ : ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).carrier),
              ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).mult ↑ρ) * Zeta23.paperFT k (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ) =
            SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.literatureRHS_chi χ k
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.EF_lit_chi' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.EF_lit_chi

@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.Contour @@LINE 27 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.rectangle_identity_chi : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N] {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N},
  χ.IsPrimitive →
    χ ≠ 1 →
      ∀ {k : ℝ → ℂ},
        ContDiff ℝ 2 k →
          HasCompactSupport k →
            ∀ {c : ℝ},
              1 < c →
                ∀ {R : ℝ},
                  7 ≤ R →
                    (∀ (s : ℂ),
                        s.im = R ∨ s.im = -R →
                          1 - c ≤ s.re → s.re ≤ c → DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ s ≠ 0) →
                      ∃ Z,
                        ↑Z = {ρ | SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.IsNontrivialZeroChi χ ρ ∧ -R < ρ.im ∧ ρ.im < R} ∧
                          RectangleIntegral'
                              (fun s => Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k s * logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ) s)
                              (↑(1 - c) - ↑R * Complex.I) (↑c + ↑R * Complex.I) =
                            ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.zeroMultChi χ ρ) * Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k ρ
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.rectangle_identity_chi' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.rectangle_identity_chi

def SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.classK : (ℝ → ℂ) → Prop :=
fun k =>
  (∀ (x : ℝ), k (-x) = k x) ∧
    ContDiff ℝ 2 k ∧ HasCompactSupport k ∧ ∃ h, ContDiff ℝ 2 h ∧ HasCompactSupport h ∧ k = Zeta23.EF.weilTest h h
@@AXIOMS

    χ ≠ 1 →
      ∀ (k : ℝ → ℂ),
        SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.archTerm_chi χ k - SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.primeSum_chi χ k =
          SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.archTerm_chi χ.primitiveCharacter k -
            SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.primeSum_chi χ.primitiveCharacter k

def SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Fline_chi : {N : ℕ} → [NeZero N] → DirichletCharacter ℂ N → (ℝ → ℂ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℂ :=
fun {N} [NeZero N] χ k c t =>
  Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k (↑c + ↑t * Complex.I) * logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ) (↑c + ↑t * Complex.I) +
      Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k (1 - ↑c - ↑t * Complex.I) *
        logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ⁻¹) (↑c + ↑t * Complex.I) +
    SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.FlineCond_chi N k c t

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_Assembly_BlockInputs_hat : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1) {P : Zeta23.Params} {T : ℝ},
  Zeta23.Assembly.BlockInputs (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) P T →
    ∃ Pm Qm p,
      ∃ (_ : Pm.PosSemidef) (hQm : Qm.IsHermitian),
        P.hat T ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Az P T) = Pm + Qm ∧
          Pm.rank ≤
              (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s1 T +
                (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s2 T ∧
            RHLinalg.rtrace Pm ≤ ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).NonIprime T) ∧
              RHLinalg.posIndex hQm ≤ p ∧
                ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).NonIprime T) + 2 * ↑p ≤
                  ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).NIprime T)

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_Assembly_BlockInputs_tilde

@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_Assembly_BlockInputs_tilde : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1) {P : Zeta23.Params} {T : ℝ},
  Zeta23.Assembly.BlockInputs (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) P T →
    ∃ p,
      ∃ (hAt : (P.tilde T ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Az P T)).IsHermitian),
        RHLinalg.posIndex hAt ≤
            (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s1 T +
                (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s2 T +
              p ∧
          (P.tilde T ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Az P T)).rank ≤
              ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).ZIprime T).ncard ∧
            ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s1 T) +
                  2 * ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).s2 T) +
                2 * ↑p ≤
              ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).NIprime T)
@@AXIOMS

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_EF_prop_EF_of_lit : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1),
  Zeta23.EF.EF_lit (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) →
    ∀ {L : ℝ},
      0 < L →
        ∀ {f g : ℝ → ℂ},
          ContDiff ℝ 2 f →
            ContDiff ℝ 2 g →
              tsupport f ⊆ Set.Icc (-(L / 2)) (L / 2) →
                tsupport g ⊆ Set.Icc (-(L / 2)) (L / 2) →
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (FourierTransform.fourier (Zeta23.EF.weilTest f g)) MeasureTheory.volume →
                    MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun τ => Zeta23.paperFT (Zeta23.EF.weilTest f g) ↑τ * ↑(Zeta23.mu τ))
                        MeasureTheory.volume →
                      (Summable fun ρ => (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Wsummand f g ↑ρ) ∧
                        MeasureTheory.Integrable
                            (fun τ =>
                              Zeta23.paperFT f ↑τ * (starRingEnd ℂ) (Zeta23.paperFT g ↑τ) *
                                ↑(Zeta23.nuX (Real.exp L) τ))
                            MeasureTheory.volume ∧
                          (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).W f g =
                            ∫ (τ : ℝ),
                              Zeta23.paperFT f ↑τ * (starRingEnd ℂ) (Zeta23.paperFT g ↑τ) * ↑(Zeta23.nuX (Real.exp L) τ)

    ∀ {A₀ : ℝ},
      1 ≤ A₀ →
        (∀ (t : ℝ), ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N t (t + 1)) ≤ A₀ * Real.log (|t| + 3)) →
          ∀ (C₁ : ℝ → ℝ),

              (∀ᶠ (T : ℝ) in Filter.atTop,
                  ∀ (r y : ℝ),
                    |y| ≤ 1 / 2 →
                      ↑r - Complex.I * ↑y ≠ 0 →
                        ‖P.phiHat T (↑r - Complex.I * ↑y)‖ ≤ Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁ T / ‖↑r - Complex.I * ↑y‖ ^ 2) →
                (∀ᶠ (T : ℝ) in Filter.atTop, 0 < P.a T) →
                  (∀ᶠ (T : ℝ) in Filter.atTop,
                      ∀ (z : ℂ), P.phiHat T ((starRingEnd ℂ) z) = (starRingEnd ℂ) (P.phiHat T z)) →
                    ∀ᶠ (T : ℝ) in Filter.atTop,
                      Zeta23.Assembly.TailInputs (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) P T
                        (Zeta23.Tail.theta0 A₀ (Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁ T) T)

  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1) {P : Zeta23.Params} {T : ℝ},
  Zeta23.Tail.T₀ ≤ T →
    2 ≤ P.L T →
      ∀ {C₁ : ℝ},
        0 ≤ C₁ →
          (∀ (r y : ℝ),
              |y| ≤ 1 / 2 →
                ↑r - Complex.I * ↑y ≠ 0 →
                  ‖P.phiHat T (↑r - Complex.I * ↑y)‖ ≤ Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁ / ‖↑r - Complex.I * ↑y‖ ^ 2) →
            ∀ {ρ : ℂ},

                  ∑ k, ‖Zeta23.Tail.uvec P T ρ k‖ ^ 2 ≤
                    (Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁) ^ 2 * P.L T * (Zeta23.Tail.distI T ρ.im ^ 3)⁻¹

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_Tail_norm_uvec_le : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1) {P : Zeta23.Params} {T : ℝ},
  0 < P.L T →
    0 < T →
      ∀ {C₁ : ℝ},
        0 ≤ C₁ →
          (∀ (r y : ℝ),
              |y| ≤ 1 / 2 →
                ↑r - Complex.I * ↑y ≠ 0 →
                  ‖P.phiHat T (↑r - Complex.I * ↑y)‖ ≤ Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁ / ‖↑r - Complex.I * ↑y‖ ^ 2) →
            ∀ {ρ : ℂ},
              ρ ∈ (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).carrier →
                Zeta23.Tail.InTail T ρ.im →
                  ∀ (k : Fin (P.d T)),
                    ‖Zeta23.Tail.uvec P T ρ k‖ ≤
                      Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁ * (|ρ.im - (T + ↑↑k * (2 * Real.pi / P.L T))| ^ 2)⁻¹

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_Tail_prop_tail : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N) (hχ : χ.IsPrimitive) (h1 : χ ≠ 1) {P : Zeta23.Params} {T A₀ C₁ : ℝ},
  Zeta23.Tail.TailHyp (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) P T A₀ C₁ →
    0 < P.a T →
      ∀ (hEt : (P.tilde T ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Ez P T)).IsHermitian)
        (hEh : (P.hat T ((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Ez P T)).IsHermitian),
        (∀ (i : Fin (P.d T)), |hEt.eigenvalues i| ≤ Zeta23.Tail.theta0 A₀ (Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁) T) ∧
          Zeta23.Tail.traceNorm hEt ≤ Zeta23.Tail.theta0 A₀ (Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁) T ∧
            Zeta23.Tail.traceNorm hEh ≤ Zeta23.Tail.theta0 A₀ (Real.exp (P.L T / 4) * C₁) T / (P.a T * P.L T)

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.Generic.chi_ZeroConfig_trivial_chain : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]

      (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N0star T₁ T₂ ∧
    (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N0star T₁ T₂ ≤
        (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N0 T₁ T₂ ∧
      (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N0 T₁ T₂ ≤
          (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N T₁ T₂ ∧
        (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N0s T₁ T₂ ≤
            (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Ns T₁ T₂ ∧
          (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Ns T₁ T₂ ≤
              (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Nd T₁ T₂ ∧
            (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).Nd T₁ T₂ ≤
              (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).N T₁ T₂

fun {N} χ s => ∃ m, s = -↑(2 * m + SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.parity χ)
@@AXIOMS

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0
@@CELL @@KIND def @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.ZetaBounds @@LINE 142 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0 : {N : ℕ} → DirichletCharacter ℂ N → ℕ → ℂ → ℂ :=
fun {N} χ M s =>
  ∑ n ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M, χ ↑n / ↑n ^ s - SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.charPartialSum χ ↑M * ↑M ^ (-s) +
    s * ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioi ↑M, SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.charPartialSum χ x * ↑x ^ (-(s + 1))
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0
@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0'
@@CELL @@KIND def @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.ZetaBoundsStrip @@LINE 212 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0' : {N : ℕ} → DirichletCharacter ℂ N → ℕ → ℂ → ℂ :=
fun {N} χ M s =>
  (∑ n ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M, χ ↑n / ↑n ^ s * -↑(Real.log ↑n) +
        SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.charPartialSum χ ↑M * (↑(Real.log ↑M) * ↑M ^ (-s)) +
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioi ↑M, SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.charPartialSum χ x * ↑x ^ (-(s + 1))) +
    s * ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioi ↑M, SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.charPartialSum χ x * ↑x ^ (-(s + 1)) * -↑(Real.log x)
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0'' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0'
@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0_eq_LFunction
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.ZetaBoundsStrip @@LINE 307 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction0_eq_LFunction : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N] {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N},
  χ ≠ 1 →
    ∀ {M : ℕ},

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.LFunction_logDeriv_partial_fraction : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]
  {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N},
  χ ≠ 1 →
    ∃ C,
      0 < C ∧
        ∀ (t : ℝ),
          6 ≤ |t| →
            ∃ Z,
              ↑Z =
                  {ρ |
                    ρ ∈ Metric.closedBall (2 + ↑t * Complex.I) (22 / 25 * (91 / 50)) ∧
                      DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ ρ = 0} ∧
                ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(analyticOrderNatAt (DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ) ρ) ≤ C * Real.log (|t| + 3) ∧
                  ∀ s ∈ Metric.closedBall (2 + ↑t * Complex.I) (3 / 2),
                    DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ s ≠ 0 →
                      ‖logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ) s -
                            ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(analyticOrderNatAt (DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ) ρ) / (s - ρ)‖ ≤
                        C * Real.log (|t| + 3)
@@AXIOMS

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.dominant_summable_chi : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N] {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N}
  {hχ : χ.IsPrimitive} {h1 : χ ≠ 1} {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} {L M : ℝ},

@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_bound_chi : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N] {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N}
  {hχ : χ.IsPrimitive} {h1 : χ ≠ 1} {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} {L M : ℝ}
  (H : SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.PWSetup (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) g0 L M)
  (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℝ) (B : ℝ) (D : ℕ),
  (∀ (j : ℕ) (w : ℂ), ‖(Polynomial.aeval w) (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.Pof H (Q j))‖ ≤ B * (1 + ‖w‖) ^ D) →
    ∀ (j : ℕ),
      D ≤ j →
        ∀ (x : ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).carrier),
          ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_chi H Q j x / ↑M ^ 2 ^ (j + 1)‖ ≤
            ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).mult ↑x) *
              (B ^ 2 * (1 + ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.wOf ↑x‖) ^ (2 * D) *
                (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.offScore g0 ↑x / M) ^ 2 ^ (D + 1))
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_bound_chi' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_bound_chi
@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_chi
@@CELL @@KIND def @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.CriterionConverse @@LINE 46 @@CONSUMES [SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_chi : {N : ℕ} →
  [inst : NeZero N] →
    {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N} →
      {hχ : χ.IsPrimitive} →
        {h1 : χ ≠ 1} →
          {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} →
            {L M : ℝ} →
              SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.PWSetup (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) g0 L M →
                (ℕ → Polynomial ℝ) → ℕ → ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).carrier → ℂ :=
fun {N} [NeZero N] {χ} {hχ} {h1} {g0} {L M} H Q j x =>
  if ↑x ∈ SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.tieSet (SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1) g0 M then 0
  else
    ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.chiZeroConfig χ hχ h1).mult ↑x) *
      Zeta23.paperFT
        (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.kWindow (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.coeffList (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.Pof H (Q j))) g0
          j)
        (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑x)
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_chi' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.fR_chi

  {k : ℝ → ℂ},

      ∀ {c : ℝ},

              ∫ (t : ℝ),

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.horizontal_vanish_chi
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Chi.Horizontal @@LINE 61 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.horizontal_vanish_chi : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N] {χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N},
  χ.IsPrimitive →
    χ ≠ 1 →
      ∀ {k : ℝ → ℂ},
        ContDiff ℝ 2 k →
          HasCompactSupport k →
            ∀ {c : ℝ},
              1 < c →
                c ≤ 3 / 2 →
                  ∀ {Cg : ℝ},
                    0 < Cg →
                      ∀ {R : ℕ → ℝ},
                        (∀ (j : ℕ),
                            ↑j + 7 ≤ R j ∧
                              R j ≤ ↑j + 8 ∧
                                ∀ (s : ℂ),
                                  s.im = R j ∨ s.im = -R j →
                                    1 / 2 ≤ s.re →
                                      s.re ≤ 2 →
                                        DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ s ≠ 0 ∧
                                          ‖logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ) s‖ ≤ Cg * Real.log (↑j + 10) ^ 2) →
                          Filter.Tendsto
                              (fun j =>
                                HIntegral
                                  (fun s =>
                                    Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k s * logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ) s)
                                  (1 - c) c (R j))
                              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                            Filter.Tendsto
                              (fun j =>
                                HIntegral
                                  (fun s =>
                                    Zeta23.WeilEF.Hfn k s * logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction χ) s)
                                  (1 - c) c (-R j))
                              Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.horizontal_vanish_chi' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.horizontal_vanish_chi

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.GRHWeil.norm_logDeriv_completed_le_of_good : ∀ {N : ℕ} [inst : NeZero N]

  ψ ≠ 1 →
    ∀ {CG Cg Lg : ℝ},
      0 ≤ CG →
        1 ≤ Lg →
          (∀ (σ t : ℝ),
              1 / 2 ≤ σ → σ ≤ 3 / 2 → ‖logDeriv ψ.gammaFactor (↑σ + ↑t * Complex.I)‖ ≤ CG * Real.log (2 + |t|)) →
            ∀ {s : ℂ},
              1 / 2 ≤ s.re →
                s.re ≤ 3 / 2 →
                  Real.log (2 + |s.im|) ≤ Lg →
                    DirichletCharacter.LFunction ψ s ≠ 0 →
                      ‖logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.LFunction ψ) s‖ ≤ Cg * Lg ^ 2 →
                        ‖logDeriv (DirichletCharacter.completedLFunction ψ) s‖ ≤ (CG + Cg) * Lg ^ 2

    HasCompactSupport k →

            0

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_bound_cfg : ∀ {C : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig} {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} {L M : ℝ}
  (H : SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.PWSetup C.toZeroConfig g0 L M) (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℝ) (B : ℝ) (D : ℕ),
  (∀ (j : ℕ) (w : ℂ), ‖(Polynomial.aeval w) (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.Pof H (Q j))‖ ≤ B * (1 + ‖w‖) ^ D) →
    ∀ (j : ℕ),
      D ≤ j →
        ∀ (x : ↑C.carrier),
          ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg H Q j x / ↑M ^ 2 ^ (j + 1)‖ ≤
            ↑(C.mult ↑x) *
              (B ^ 2 * (1 + ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.wOf ↑x‖) ^ (2 * D) *
                (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.offScore g0 ↑x / M) ^ 2 ^ (D + 1))

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg
@@CELL @@KIND def @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.Converse @@LINE 43 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg : {C : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig} →
  {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} →
    {L M : ℝ} → SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.PWSetup C.toZeroConfig g0 L M → (ℕ → Polynomial ℝ) → ℕ → ↑C.carrier → ℂ :=
fun {C} {g0} {L M} H Q j x =>
  if ↑x ∈ SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.tieSet C.toZeroConfig g0 M then 0
  else
    ↑(C.mult ↑x) *
      Zeta23.paperFT
        (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.kWindow (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.coeffList (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.Pof H (Q j))) g0
          j)
        (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑x)
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_norm_le_cfg : ∀ {C : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig} {g0 : ℝ → ℝ} {L M : ℝ}
  (H : SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.PWSetup C.toZeroConfig g0 L M) (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℝ) (B : ℝ) (D : ℕ),
  (∀ (j : ℕ) (w : ℂ), ‖(Polynomial.aeval w) (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.Pof H (Q j))‖ ≤ B * (1 + ‖w‖) ^ D) →
    ∀ (j : ℕ) (x : ↑C.carrier),
      ↑x ∉ SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.tieSet C.toZeroConfig g0 M →
        ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.fR_cfg H Q j x / ↑M ^ 2 ^ (j + 1)‖ ≤
          ↑(C.mult ↑x) *
            (B ^ 2 * (1 + ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.wOf ↑x‖) ^ (2 * D) *
              (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.offScore g0 ↑x / M) ^ 2 ^ (j + 1))

def SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.zetaWeilConfig : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig :=

  rhs := fun k =>
    SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.poleTerm k - SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.primeSum k + SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.archTerm k,
  ef := SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.zetaWeilConfig._proof_2, count := Zeta23.RvM.zetaZeroConfig_local_count,
  target := RiemannHypothesis, target_iff := SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.zetaWeilConfig._proof_3 }
